---

## 1. Mandatory test-case schema

Every executable test case contains: Test ID; Requirement; Purpose; Provenance (`ND-*` + evidence-class + `EV-*`); Preconditions; Fixture rows; Setup; Action; Independent oracle; Expected result; Failure injection; Expected failure; Cleanup; Evidence artifact.

Rejected: title-only; “verify checkpoint”; “run all prior tests”; “compare expected result”; earlier-revision reference; unstated fixture; production function as its own oracle.

Named preconditions: `PRE-ISOLATED`, `PRE-SCHEMA8-BASE`, `PRE-MIGRATING`, `PRE-PREFLIGHT-PASS`, `PRE-RUNTIME-UNKNOWN`. Cleanup: `CLN-ROLLBACK`, `CLN-DISPOSE`, `CLN-NONE-SPEC`.

## 2. Test ID namespaces

`CP-*`, `RC-*`, `MAP-*`, `STA-*`, `STB-*`, `EVR-*`, `LVP-*`, `RTR-*`, `ACL-FN-*`, `ACL-TBL-*`, `ACL-COL-*`, `FAIL-*`. All IDs unique; first/last/total in §27.

## 22. Independent oracle requirement

Construct expected digests/mappings/classifications/privileges independently. Do not call production classifier, production digest, or production preflight as oracle. Do not infer privileges solely from has_*_privilege. Use literal ACL expansion, set calculus, and offline SHA-256 (§3).

## 23. Execution isolation requirements

Future execution (not authorized now) must use disposable DB/container; local-only endpoint; isolated names; rollback; no Compose prod DB; no live provider/model; no n8n; no external network; no paid ops; no real credentials. This phase defines requirements only.
